###############################################################################
# How a character's nature takes shape
#
# Part 1: the cold heart       coldheart.1 (was sketchy.1 in
#                              psychopath_events.txt)
# Part 2: the shaping of a     persona.1 - 73 (was 1000 - 1071 in
#   character                  personality_traits_events.txt)
# Part 3: trait               traitnote.150 - 322 (was 38150 - 38322 in
#   notifications              trait_notification.txt)
# Part 4: personality         traits.100 - 855 (was 3000 - 3755 in
#   trait effects              traits_effects_events.txt)
# Part 5: life with a trait   traitlife.100 - 267 (was 65000 - 65167 in
#                              various_traits_events.txt)
###############################################################################

namespace = coldheart

###############################################################################
# Part 1: the cold heart (namespace coldheart)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: the Old World faiths
#
# A youth of the Old World faiths, raised by a cruel, drunken or maddened
# parent, finds one day that the pain of others means nothing to them. It
# was an MTTH event of eight years; it now rolls on the bi-yearly pulse
# between 14 and 18, so a youth gets two or three rolls. The followers of
# Shallya and the Lady are spared.
#
#   coldheart.1   A servant begs leave to visit his dying mother
#
# Fired from: on_bi_yearly_pulse coldheart_youth list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt).
# Loc: localisation/00_WHG_personality_events.csv
###############################################################################

# coldheart.1 A youth with a cruel upbringing refuses a servant's plea and
# feels nothing. Take the psychopath trait, or fight the coldness and maybe
# win (a lost fight also leaves them stressed).
character_event = {
	id = coldheart.1
	desc = EVTDESCcoldheart1
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_bi_yearly_pulse coldheart_youth list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	min_age = 14
	max_age = 18
	religion_group = old_world_group

	trigger = {
		NOR = {
			religion = grail
			religion = shallyan
			trait = kind
			trait = just
			trait = psychopath
			trait = temperate
			trait = patient
		}
		OR = {
			father = {
				NOR = { trait = kind trait = temperate trait = patient trait = just trait = humble }
				calc_true_if = {
					amount = 4
					trait = cruel
					trait = wroth
					trait = psychopath
					trait = envious
					trait = impaler
					trait = lunatic_1
					trait = lunatic_2
					trait = lunatic_3
					trait = possessed
					trait = drunkard
					trait = stressed
					trait = depressed
					trait = greedy
					trait = deceitful
					trait = kinslayer
					trait = gluttonous
				}
			}
			mother = {
				NOR = { trait = kind trait = temperate trait = patient trait = just trait = humble }
				calc_true_if = {
					amount = 5
					trait = cruel
					trait = wroth
					trait = psychopath
					trait = envious
					trait = impaler
					trait = lunatic_1
					trait = lunatic_2
					trait = lunatic_3
					trait = possessed
					trait = drunkard
					trait = stressed
					trait = depressed
					trait = greedy
					trait = deceitful
					trait = kinslayer
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = lustful
				}
			}
		}
		calc_true_if = {
			amount = 2
			NOT = { trait = honest }
			NOT = { trait = just }
			NOT = { trait = charitable }
			NOT = { trait = humble }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 3 trait = cruel }
		modifier = { factor = 3 trait = wroth }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = rowdy }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = haughty }
	}

	option = { # Their pain is nothing to me
		name = EVTOPTAcoldheart1
		add_trait = psychopath
		ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
	}
	option = { # Fight the coldness
		name = EVTOPTBcoldheart1
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				add_trait = psychopath
				if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } } add_trait = stressed }
			}
			50 = { custom_tooltip = { text = TTcoldheart1_spared } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 2: the shaping of a character (namespace persona)
# Namespace: persona (was 1000 - 1071 in personality_traits_events.txt:
#   persona.N = old id minus 999)
# Faction: Generic
#
# The vanilla adult personality events. Every five years a grown character
# may face a small choice (a feast, a beggar, a poacher, an envoy's
# question) that tips them towards one trait or its opposite, with odds set
# by the traits they already have. A character with six personality traits
# may lose one instead. The faithful of Ormazd get their own lust event,
# and may vow to learn the Scriptures of the Prophets by heart (hafiz).
#
#   persona.1 - 20      Gaining a trait (persona.3, the second gluttony
#                       event, was a copy of persona.2 and is dropped)
#   persona.29 - 59     Losing a trait
#   persona.61          Araby: the lust event
#   persona.71 - 73     Araby: the hafiz vow and its keeping (72 was MTTH,
#                       now a year or two after the vow; 73 is the hidden
#                       check that waits or lets the vow lapse)
#
# Fired from: the on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt), with
#   the other trait events; persona.32 also from WHG_health_events (the
#   physician's warning to a glutton); persona.73 from persona.71 and
#   itself; persona.72 from persona.73.
# Notifications: 38251 - 38277 in trait_notification.txt.
# Saved: character flags aspiring_hafiz_flag, had_event_1013; character
#   modifiers aspiring_hafiz, well_fed.
# Loc: localisation/00_WHG_personality_events.csv; persona.71 - 72 keep the
#   mod's Araby keys EVTDESC1070 - 1071 (00_vanilla_replace_soi.csv and
#   the other replacement files).
###############################################################################

namespace = persona

# persona.1 Gaining Lustful or Chaste - male: a choice shapes the character, with odds set by their other traits.
character_event = {
	id = persona.1
	desc = EVTDESCpersona1
	picture = "GFX_evt_lovers"

	min_age = 16
	only_men = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		NOT = { religion = ormazd }
		NOT = { religion = kitabid }
		NOT = { trait = chaste }
		# sexless
		NOR = { culture_group = orc_group culture_group = goblin_group }
		NOT = { trait = lustful }
		NOT = { trait = celibate }
		NOT = { trait = homosexual }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = hedonist }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApersona1
		if = {
			limit = { trait = honest NOT = { trait = slothful } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = lustful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.272 #Notify Lustful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = slothful NOT = { trait = honest } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = lustful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.272 #Notify Lustful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = honest } NOT = { trait = slothful } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = lustful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.272 #Notify Lustful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = honest trait = slothful }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = lustful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.272 #Notify Lustful
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpersona1
		if = {
			limit = { trait = arbitrary NOT = { trait = proud } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = chaste
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.274 #Notify Chaste
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = proud NOT = { trait = arbitrary } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = chaste
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.274 #Notify Chaste
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = proud } NOT = { trait = arbitrary } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = chaste
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.274 #Notify Chaste
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = proud trait = arbitrary }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = chaste
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.274 #Notify Chaste
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# persona.2 Gaining Gluttonous or Temperate: a choice shapes the character, with odds set by their other traits.
character_event = {
	id = persona.2
	desc = EVTDESCpersona2
	picture = "GFX_evt_feast"

	min_age = 16

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
		NOT = { trait = temperate }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = hedonist }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = lustful }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = slothful }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApersona2
		add_character_modifier = { name = well_fed years = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = lustful }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = gluttonous
				gain_weight_small_effect = yes
				weight_trait_check_effect = yes

				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.277 #Notify Gluttonous
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = chaste NOT = { trait = lustful } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = gluttonous
				gain_weight_small_effect = yes
				weight_trait_check_effect = yes
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.277 #Notify Gluttonous
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = chaste } NOT = { trait = lustful } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = gluttonous
				gain_weight_small_effect = yes
				weight_trait_check_effect = yes
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.277 #Notify Gluttonous
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpersona2
		if = {
			limit = { trait = zealous NOT = { trait = greedy } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = temperate
				lose_weight_small_effect = yes
				weight_trait_check_effect = yes
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.276 #Notify Temperate
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = greedy NOT = { trait = zealous } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = temperate
				lose_weight_small_effect = yes
				weight_trait_check_effect = yes
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.276 #Notify Temperate
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = greedy } NOT = { trait = zealous } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = temperate
				lose_weight_small_effect = yes
				weight_trait_check_effect = yes
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.276 #Notify Temperate
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = greedy trait = zealous }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = temperate
				lose_weight_small_effect = yes
				weight_trait_check_effect = yes
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.276 #Notify Temperate
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# persona.4 Gaining Charitable or Greedy: a choice shapes the character, with odds set by their other traits.

	id = persona.4
	desc = EVTDESCpersona4

		NOT = { trait = greedy }
		NOT = { trait = charitable }
	}

		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = kind }
	}

			limit = { trait = honest NOT = { trait = envious } }

				chance = 70

				add_trait = charitable

					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.273 #Notify Charitable
					}
				}
			}
		}

			limit = { trait = envious NOT = { trait = honest } }

				chance = 20

				add_trait = charitable
				hidden_tooltip = {

						id = traitnote.273 #Notify Charitable

					}

# persona.5 Gaining Slothful or Diligent: a choice shapes the character, with odds set by their other traits.
character_event = {
	id = persona.5
	desc = EVTDESCpersona5
	picture = "GFX_evt_drunk"

	min_age = 16

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		NOT = { trait = diligent }
		NOT = { trait = slothful }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = lustful }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = hedonist }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApersona5
		if = {
			limit = { trait = cruel NOT = { trait = ambitious } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = slothful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.251 #Notify Slothful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = ambitious NOT = { trait = cruel } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = slothful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.251 #Notify Slothful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } NOT = { trait = cruel } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = slothful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.251 #Notify Slothful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = ambitious trait = cruel }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = slothful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.251 #Notify Slothful
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpersona5
		if = { limit = { trait = depressed trait = stressed } health = -0.5 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = depressed NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			random = { chance = 60 add_trait = stressed }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = stressed NOT = { trait = depressed } }
			random = { chance = 60 add_trait = depressed }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = stressed trait = depressed } }
			random = { chance = 60 add_trait = stressed }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = patient NOT = { trait = cynical } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = diligent
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.256 #Notify Diligent
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = cynical NOT = { trait = patient } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = diligent
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.256 #Notify Diligent
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = cynical } NOT = { trait = patient } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = diligent
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.256 #Notify Diligent
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = cynical trait = patient }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = diligent
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.256 #Notify Diligent
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# persona.6 Gaining Kind or Envious - female: a choice shapes the character, with odds set by their other traits.
character_event = {
	id = persona.6
	desc = EVTDESCpersona6
	picture = "GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal"

	only_women = yes
	min_age = 16

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		NOT = { trait = envious }
		NOT = { trait = kind }
		NOT = { trait = homosexual }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = proud }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = charitable }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApersona6
		if = {
			limit = { trait = gregarious NOT = { trait = wroth } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = envious
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.271 #Notify Envious
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = envious
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.271 #Notify Envious
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = wroth } NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = envious
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.271 #Notify Envious
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth trait = gregarious }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = envious
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.271 #Notify Envious
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpersona6
		if = {
			limit = { trait = gregarious NOT = { trait = wroth } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.268 #Notify Kind
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.268 #Notify Kind
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = wroth } NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.268 #Notify Kind
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth trait = gregarious }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.268 #Notify Kind
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# persona.7 Gaining Kind or Envious - male: a choice shapes the character, with odds set by their other traits.
character_event = {
	id = persona.7
	desc = EVTDESCpersona7
	picture = "GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal"

	only_men = yes
	min_age = 16

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		NOT = { trait = envious }
		NOT = { trait = kind }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = proud }

# persona.13 Gaining Ambitious or Content: a choice shapes the character, with odds set by their other traits.
character_event = {
	id = persona.13
	desc = EVTDESCpersona13
	picture = "GFX_evt_council"

	min_age = 16

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		NOT = { trait = ambitious }
		NOT = { trait = content }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 15 any_owned_bloodline = { has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_ambition } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = proud }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApersona13

			limit = { any_owned_bloodline = { has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_ambition } }

		}

				chance = 70

				add_trait = content
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.279 #Notify Content
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = lustful NOT = { trait = patient } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = content
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.279 #Notify Content
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = patient } NOT = { trait = lustful } }

				add_trait = content

# persona.14 Gaining Arbitrary or Just: a choice shapes the character, with odds set by their other traits.
character_event = {
	id = persona.14
	desc = EVTDESCpersona14
	picture = "GFX_evt_beheading"
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy

	min_age = 16

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		NOT = { trait = arbitrary }
		NOT = { trait = just }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = had_event_1013 }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = deceitful }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = cruel }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = honest }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = had_event_1013
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApersona14
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = stressed trait = depressed } } health = 0.5 }
		if = { limit = { trait = stressed NOT = { trait = depressed } } remove_trait = stressed }
		if = { limit = { trait = depressed } remove_trait = depressed }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = diligent NOT = { trait = shy } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = arbitrary
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.266 #Notify Arbitrary
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = shy NOT = { trait = diligent } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = arbitrary
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.266 #Notify Arbitrary
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = diligent } NOT = { trait = shy } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = arbitrary
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.266 #Notify Arbitrary
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = diligent trait = shy }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = arbitrary
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.266 #Notify Arbitrary
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpersona14
		if = {
			limit = { trait = honest NOT = { trait = content } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = just
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.267 #Notify Just
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = content NOT = { trait = honest } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = just
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.267 #Notify Just
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = honest } NOT = { trait = content } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = just
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.267 #Notify Just
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = honest trait = content }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = just
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.267 #Notify Just
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

# persona.15 Gaining Cynical or Zealous: a choice shapes the character, with odds set by their other traits.
character_event = {
	id = persona.15
	desc = EVTDESCpersona15
	picture = "GFX_evt_bishop"

	min_age = 16
	religion_group = old_world_group

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		NOT = { trait = cynical }
		NOT = { trait = zealous }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 1.7 OR = { trait = quick trait = shrewd } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = genius }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { trait = slow trait = dull } }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = imbecile }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = detached_priest }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = mastermind_theologian }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = scholarly_theologian }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApersona15
		if = {
			limit = { trait = patient NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = zealous
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.269 #Notify Zealous
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = paranoid NOT = { trait = patient } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = zealous
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.269 #Notify Zealous
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = patient } NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = zealous
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.269 #Notify Zealous
					}
				}
			}
		}

				hidden_tooltip = {

				chance = 70

				add_trait = cynical
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.258 #Notify Cynical
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = slothful NOT = { trait = wroth } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = cynical
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.258 #Notify Cynical
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = wroth } NOT = { trait = slothful } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = cynical
				hidden_tooltip = {

				}
			}

# persona.29 Losing Lustful - male: a personality trait fades (personality_traits = 6, so a crowded character sheds one).
character_event = {
	id = persona.29
	desc = EVTDESCpersona29
	picture = "GFX_evt_lovers"

	min_age = 16
	only_men = yes
	only_capable = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		trait = lustful
		personality_traits = 6
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 personality_traits = 6 }
	}

	desc = EVTDESCpersona30
	picture = "GFX_evt_lovers"

	min_age = 16
	only_capable = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

		trait = chaste
		personality_traits = 6
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 personality_traits = 6 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApersona30
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { is_woman_trigger = yes trait = homosexual }
				AND = { is_woman_trigger = no NOT = { trait = homosexual } }
			}
		}
		remove_trait = chaste
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpersona30
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { is_woman_trigger = yes NOT = { trait = homosexual } }
				AND = { is_woman_trigger = no trait = homosexual }
			}
		}
		remove_trait = chaste
	}
}

# persona.61 Araby: the lust event for the faithful of Ormazd, where resisting earns piety.
character_event = {
	id = persona.61
	desc = EVTDESCpersona61
	picture = "GFX_evt_lovers"

	min_age = 16
	only_capable = yes
	religion_group = ormazd_gods_group

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		OR = { religion = ormazd religion = kitabid }
		NOT = { trait = lustful }
		NOT = { trait = homosexual }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = hedonist }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApersona61
		if = {
			limit = { trait = honest NOT = { trait = slothful } }
			random = {
				chance = 70

				add_trait = lustful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.272 #Notify Lustful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = slothful NOT = { trait = honest } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = lustful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.272 #Notify Lustful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = honest } NOT = { trait = slothful } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = lustful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.272 #Notify Lustful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = honest trait = slothful }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = lustful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.272 #Notify Lustful
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpersona61
		piety = 10
	}
}

# persona.71 Araby: a Master Curator recites the Scriptures of the Prophets; vow to learn them by heart (aspiring_hafiz), which leads to persona.72 through persona.73.
character_event = {
	id = persona.71
	desc = "EVTDESC1070"
	picture = GFX_evt_monk
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = ormazd_gods_group

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse random list (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt)

	trigger = {
		NOT = { personality_traits = 5 }
		OR = { religion = ormazd religion = kitabid }
		NOT = { trait = hafiz }
		NOT = { trait = inbred }
		is_dumb_trigger = no
		NOT = { trait = infirm }
		NOT = { has_alternate_start_parameter = { key = religion_names value = random } }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = scholar }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = zealous }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = cynical }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = slothful }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = content }
	}

	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1070"
		set_character_flag = aspiring_hafiz_flag
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = scholar trait = zealous } }
			character_event = { id = persona.73 days = 500 random = 200 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = cynical trait = slothful trait = content } }
			character_event = { id = persona.73 days = 900 random = 400 }
		}
		else = { character_event = { id = persona.73 days = 700 random = 300 } }
		add_character_modifier = { name = aspiring_hafiz duration = -1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTB1070"
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = slothful } }
			random = {
				chance = 20
				add_trait = slothful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.251 #Notify Slothful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = slothful } piety = -10 }
	}
}

# persona.72 The vow kept: the character recites the Scriptures before a Master Curator and becomes a hafiz. Chained from persona.71 through persona.73 (was MTTH 12 months after a year).
character_event = {
	id = persona.72
	desc = "EVTDESC1071"
	picture = GFX_evt_monk
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
	has_character_flag = aspiring_hafiz_flag

	is_triggered_only = yes # persona.73 (a year or two after the vow)

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = aspiring_hafiz
		OR = { religion = ormazd religion = kitabid }
		NOT = { trait = hafiz }
	}

	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1071"
		add_trait = hafiz
		remove_character_modifier = aspiring_hafiz
		clr_character_flag = aspiring_hafiz_flag
	}
}

# persona.73 The vow: hidden check before persona.72. If the vow-taker is a
# prisoner, incapable or too young it waits half a year and checks again; if
# they have left the faith or lost the modifier the vow lapses (modifier and
# flag removed).
# Scope: the vow-taker. Fired from persona.71 and from itself.
character_event = {
	id = persona.73

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # persona.71, persona.73

	has_character_flag = aspiring_hafiz_flag

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { religion = ormazd religion = kitabid }
				NOT = { trait = hafiz }
				has_character_modifier = aspiring_hafiz
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					prisoner = no
					is_incapable = no
					age = 16
				}
				character_event = { id = persona.72 }
			}
			else = { character_event = { id = persona.73 days = 180 } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_character_modifier = aspiring_hafiz
			clr_character_flag = aspiring_hafiz_flag
		}
	}
}
###############################################################################
# Part 3: Trait notifications (namespace traitnote)
# Namespace: traitnote (was 38150 - 38322 in trait_notification.txt;
#   traitnote.N is the old id minus 38000)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: every character
#
# The small notices that tell a player that their child (traitnote.150 -
# traitnote.183, shown to the parent, the child in FromFrom) or they
# themselves (traitnote.250 - traitnote.322) have gained or lost a trait.
#
#   A child gains a trait              traitnote.150 - traitnote.183
#   I gain or lose a trait             traitnote.250 - traitnote.322
#
# Dropped: the commented-out child notices 38182, 38188 and 38190 (no
#   callers).
# Callers: over 80 event, decision and effect files fire these notices
#   right after they change a trait (see docs/events/event_database.csv).
###############################################################################

namespace = traitnote

	notification = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = OK
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 4: Personality trait effects (namespace traits)
# Namespace: traits (was 3000 - 3755 in traits_effects_events.txt;
#   traits.N is the old id minus 2900)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: every character
#
# Small slice-of-life events that let a ruler's personality traits matter:
# the Seven Virtues and Seven Sins, honest and deceitful, brave and craven,
# paranoid, cynical and zealous, and the lifestyle traits (hunter,
# falconer, poet, mystic, scholar, gardener, theologian, duelist,
# torturer and more). Each gives or tests a trait, moves opinions, or
# costs and earns a little gold, piety or prestige. A few branches answer
# to the Old World faiths and a few to Ormazd.
#
#   Lust and chastity                  traits.100 - traits.135
#   Gluttony                           traits.140 - traits.157
#   Greed and charity                  traits.160 - traits.197
#   Sloth and diligence                traits.200 - traits.226
#   Envy and kindness                  traits.240 - traits.277
#   Wrath and patience                 traits.280 - traits.308
#   Pride and humility                 traits.320 - traits.358
#   Deceit and honesty                 traits.360 - traits.382
#   Courage and cowardice              traits.400 - traits.436
#   Shyness, ambition, contentment     traits.440 - traits.497
#   Arbitrary and just                 traits.500 - traits.538
#   Cynicism and zeal                  traits.540 - traits.578
#   Paranoia and trust                 traits.580 - traits.617
#   Lifestyle traits                   traits.620 - traits.807
#   Gregarious courts and vassals      traits.810 - traits.825
#   Ormazd branches                    traits.830 - traits.855
#
# Fixes: the two `break` effects became if/else (traits.621, traits.696);
#   the Ormazd desc of traits.360 and the option of traits.630 got proper
#   keys; a vanilla pagan check on traits.330 was removed; Pope, Church,
#   mosque, imam, zakat, Allah, St. Paul and St. Peter texts now speak of
#   the high priests, temples, magi, alms, Ormazd and the gods.
# Added: a feast at traits.140 may, once a lifetime and only if no
#   cookbook is held, leave a decent_cookbook (5%).
# Dropped: commented-out events 3264, 3283, 3284 and 3285.
# Fired from: on_bi_yearly_pulse and on_five_year_pulse random_events
#   (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt), and from each other.
# Loc: localisation/00_WHG_personality_events.csv
###############################################################################

namespace = traits

# traits.100 Lustful positive
character_event = {
	id = traits.100
	desc = EVTDESCtraits100
	picture = GFX_evt_brothel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = lustful
		in_command = no
		NOT = { has_job_title = job_spiritual }
		any_courtier = { age = 16 prisoner = no has_job_title = job_spiritual }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = greedy }

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = charitable }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits100
		piety = 10
		remove_trait = lustful
		job_spiritual = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_approves who = ROOT years = 5 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits100
		piety = -5
		random = {
			chance = 15
			add_trait = deceitful
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.255 } }
		}
		job_spiritual = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_lying_about_confession who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# traits.101 Lustful negative
character_event = {
	id = traits.101
	desc = EVTDESCtraits101
	picture = GFX_evt_brothel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = lustful
		in_command = no
		OR = {
			any_courtier = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				trait = lustful
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
			}
			spouse = { is_alive = yes is_abroad = no NOT = { trait = lustful } }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = envious }

		modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = charitable }

		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = kind }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits101
		scaled_wealth = -0.10
	}
}

# traits.105 Lustful positive
character_event = {
	id = traits.105
	desc = EVTDESCtraits105
	picture = GFX_evt_brothel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	only_men = yes
	religion_group = old_world_group
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = lustful
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = wounded trait = craven }
		any_courtier = {
			is_woman_trigger = yes
			age = 16
			prisoner = no
			NOR = {
				age = 35
				trait = ugly
				is_close_relative = ROOT
				trait = incapable
				is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = wroth }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = proud }

		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = patient }
		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = humble }
	}
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				is_woman_trigger = yes
				prisoner = no
				NOR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					age = 35
					trait = ugly
					trait = incapable
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { is_attractive_trigger = yes }
			save_event_target_as = scoped_lady
		}

		random = {
			chance = 10
			add_trait = wounded
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.280 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits105
		trigger = { is_married = no }
		prestige = 15
		event_target:scoped_lady = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_tourney_boost who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}

		random = {
			chance = 10
			add_trait = wounded
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.280 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits105
		trigger = { is_married = yes }
		prestige = 15
		spouse = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_tourney_boost who = ROOT years = 5 } }
		random = {
			chance = 10
			add_trait = wounded
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.280 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits105
		prestige = -5
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = berserker } }
			random = {
				chance = 10
				add_trait = craven
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.257 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

### CHASTE ###

# traits.120 Chaste positive
character_event = {
	id = traits.120
	desc = EVTDESCtraits120
	picture = GFX_evt_bishop
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		trait = chaste
		NOR = { has_landed_title = k_papal_state trait = excommunicated }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = slothful }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = proud }

		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = diligent }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = humble }
		modifier = { factor = 2.5 NOT = { num_of_children = 1 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits120
		trigger = {
			religion_group = old_world_group
			NOT = { lifestyle_traits = 1 }
			is_married = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		piety = 20
		add_trait = celibate
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits120
		tooltip_info = cynical
		trigger = { trait = cynical }
		remove_trait = chaste
		piety = -5
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits120
		any_courtier = {
			limit = { age = 16 in_command = no OR = { trait = lustful trait = hedonist } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_despise_chaste who = ROOT years = 1 }
		}
		piety = 10
		prestige = 5
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraits120
		piety = 5
	}
}

# traits.121 Chaste Positive (create character)
character_event = {
	id = traits.121
	desc = EVTDESCtraits121
	picture = GFX_evt_bishop
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group
	ai = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = chaste
		in_command = no
		NOR = { trait = excommunicated trait = cynical }

			age = 18
			is_pregnant = no
			fertility = 0.4
			NOR = { trait = celibate age = 35 trait = homosexual is_close_relative = ROOT }
			has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_admire_chaste }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_woman_trigger = yes
				age = 18
				is_pregnant = no
				fertility = 0.4
				NOR = { trait = celibate age = 35 trait = homosexual is_close_relative = ROOT }
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_admire_chaste }
			}
			save_event_target_as = lovely_lady
		}
	}
	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = wroth }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = proud }

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = patient }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = humble }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits126
		event_target:lovely_lady = { impregnate_cuckoo = ROOT }
		add_trait = stressed
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits126
		event_target:lovely_lady = {
			remove_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_admire_chaste }
		}

# traits.135 Chaste negative
character_event = {
	id = traits.135
	desc = EVTDESCtraits135
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	only_men = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = chaste
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = { tier = BARON tier = count tier = duke tier = king tier = emperor }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = proud }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = weak }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = poet }
		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = scholar }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = mystic }
	}
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes age = 16 NOR = { age = 35 is_close_relative = ROOT } }
			save_event_target_as = scoped_lady
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits135
		trigger = { is_married = no }
		random = {
			chance = 25
			add_trait = lustful
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.272 } }
		}
		event_target:scoped_lady = {
			add_trait = lustful
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.272 } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_infatuated who = ROOT years = 3 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits135
		trigger = { is_married = yes }
		random = { chance = 5 remove_trait = chaste }
		spouse = {
			add_trait = lustful
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.272 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits135
		scaled_wealth = -0.25
		prestige = -15
		random = { chance = 25 ROOT = { remove_trait = chaste } }
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = traits.140
	desc = EVTDESCtraits140
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = gluttonous
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = { trait = charitable trait = gregarious stewardship = 8 }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.3 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = slothful }

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = charitable }
		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = diligent }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits140
		trigger = { NOT = { personality_traits = 5 } }
		add_trait = greedy
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits140
		scaled_wealth = -0.05
		if = { # rare: the cook writes the feast down, once a lifetime
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = feast_cookbook_written }
				NOT = { any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = cooking } }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 5
				set_character_flag = feast_cookbook_written
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPtraits140 }
				add_artifact = decent_cookbook
			}
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { age = 16 NOT = { trait = incapable } prisoner = no }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_enjoyed_feast who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# traits.170 Greedy negative
character_event = {
	id = traits.170
	desc = EVTDESCtraits170
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = greedy
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = craven }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 20
			prisoner = no
			NOR = { age = 70 character = ROOT trait = incapable }
			OR = {
				is_female = no
				has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
				has_religion_feature = religion_equal
				has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bon
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = wroth }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = proud }
		modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = patient }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
	}
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 20
				prisoner = no
				NOR = { age = 70 character = ROOT trait = incapable }
				OR = {
					is_female = no
					has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
					has_religion_feature = religion_equal
					has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bon
				}
			}
			preferred_limit = { trait = hunter }
			preferred_limit = { trait = brave }
			preferred_limit = { NOT = { trait = craven } }
			save_event_target_as = hunting_partner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits170
		ai_chance = { factor = 99 }
		show_portrait = event_target:hunting_partner
		event_target:hunting_partner = {
			show_scope_change = no
			character_event = { id = traits.171 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits171 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits170
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = content } } add_trait = content }
		if = { limit = { trait = content NOT = { trait = slothful } } add_trait = slothful }
		if = { limit = { trait = content trait = slothful } prestige = -15 }
	}
}

					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes

					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
				}
			}

				is_ruler = yes
				any_vassal = {
					age = 16
					prisoner = no
					NOR = {
						is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
						trait = incapable
						opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
	}

			any_courtier = {
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				NOR = {
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes

					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
				}
			}
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				NOR = {
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					trait = incapable
					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 0 }
				}
			}
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = charitable_to_you }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits180
		trigger = {
			is_ruler = yes
			any_vassal = {
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				NOR = {
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					trait = incapable

				}
			}

		}

# traits.181 Caring for sick and infirm
character_event = {
	id = traits.181
	desc = EVTDESCtraits181
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = charitable
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		any_courtier = {
			prisoner = no
			OR = { is_ill = yes health_traits = 1 }
			NOR = {
				is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
				trait = incapable
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = charitable_to_you }
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.05 trait = slothful }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				OR = { is_ill = yes health_traits = 1 }
				NOR = {
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					trait = incapable
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = charitable_to_you }
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = ill_courtier
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits181
		event_target:ill_courtier = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = charitable_to_you } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits181
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = proud personality_traits = 6 } }
		opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = charitable_to_you }
		add_trait = humble
		piety = 10
		event_target:ill_courtier = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = charitable_to_you } }
	}
}

# traits.240 Envy someone's martial abilities...
character_event = {
	id = traits.240
	desc = EVTDESCtraits240
	picture = GFX_evt_melee

	only_playable = yes
	only_men = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = envious
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			NOT = { demesne_size = 1 }
			prisoner = no
			is_female = no
			martial = ROOT
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits240
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				NOT = { demesne_size = 1 }
				prisoner = no
				is_female = no
				martial = ROOT
			}
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = envious_neg years = 10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { personality_traits = 6 trait = proud trait = wroth } }
			random_list = {
				50 = { }
				30 = {
					add_trait = proud
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.261 } }
				}
				20 = {
					add_trait = wroth
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.254 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits240
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				NOT = { demesne_size = 1 }
				prisoner = no
				is_female = no
				martial = ROOT
			}
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = envious_pos years = 10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { personality_traits = 6 trait = deceitful trait = humble } }
			random_list = {
				50 = { }
				30 = {
					add_trait = deceitful
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.255 } }
				}
				20 = {
					add_trait = humble
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.260 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits240
		trigger = { OR = { trait = ambitious trait = proud trait = brave trait = zealous } }
		change_martial = 1
		random = {
			chance = 15
			add_trait = stressed
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.282 } }
		}
	}
}

# traits.245 I have no children, and other women have it...
character_event = {
	id = traits.245
	desc = EVTDESCtraits245
	picture = GFX_evt_child_play

	max_age = 44
	prisoner = no
	only_women = yes
	capable_only = yes
	war = no
	is_married = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = envious
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes num_of_children = 1 }
		any_courtier = {
			is_woman_trigger = yes
			age = 16
			NOT = { demesne_size = 1 }
			ROOT = { age_diff = { who = PREV years = 5 } }
			prisoner = no
			num_of_children = 1
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_woman_trigger = yes
				age = 16
				NOT = { demesne_size = 1 }
				ROOT = { age_diff = { who = PREV years = 5 } }
				prisoner = no
				num_of_children = 1
			}
			save_event_target_as = young_mother
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits245
		event_target:young_mother = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = envious_neg years = 10 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { personality_traits = 6 trait = proud trait = wroth } }
			random_list = {
				50 = { }
				30 = {
					add_trait = proud
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.261 } }
				}
				20 = {
					add_trait = wroth
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.254 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits245
		event_target:young_mother = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = envious_pos years = 10 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { personality_traits = 6 trait = deceitful trait = humble } }
			random_list = {
				50 = { }
				30 = {
					add_trait = deceitful
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.255 } }
				}
				20 = {
					add_trait = humble
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.260 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits245
		trigger = { OR = { trait = ambitious trait = proud trait = brave } }
		fertility = 0.1
		random = {
			chance = 33
			add_trait = stressed
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.282 } }
		}
	}
}

		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = diligent }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits285

			limit = { age = 16 trait = trusting NOT = { stewardship = 6 } }
			character_event = { id = traits.286 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits286 }
		}
	}

	prisoner = no

		in_command = no
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = drunkard }
		modifier = { factor = 10 has_ambition = obj_atone_for_sins }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits302
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = gluttonous } }
		health = 0.25
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits302
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = drunkard } }
		health = 0.25
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits302
		trigger = { OR = { trait = drunkard trait = gluttonous } }
		if = { limit = { trait = drunkard } remove_trait = drunkard }
		if = { limit = { trait = gluttonous } remove_trait = gluttonous }
	}
}

character_event = {

	picture = GFX_evt_knight_kneeling

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group
	war = no
	only_men = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

		trait = patient
		in_command = no
		NOR = {
			is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
			trait = cynical
			has_character_modifier = mercy_to_sinners
		}
		piety = 50
		learning = 7
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			is_female = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { trait = cynical is_heretic = yes NOT = { piety = 25 } }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2.5 piety = 100 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = zealous }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits305
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				is_female = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				OR = { trait = cynical is_heretic = yes NOT = { piety = 25 } }
			}
			character_event = { id = traits.306 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits306 }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = mercy_to_sinners duration = -1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits305
		piety = 5
	}
}

		job_spymaster = {
			intrigue = ROOT
			NOT = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = talent_recognized } }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = ambitious }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits346
		job_spymaster = {
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = talent_recognized years = 5 }
			letter_event = { id = traits.344 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits344 }
		}
	}
	option = {

	}
}

# traits.360 Deceitful hides their lack of martial skill
character_event = {
	id = traits.360
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCtraits360 #Hide martial
		trigger = { religion_group = old_world_group }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCBtraits360 trigger = { NOT = { religion_group = old_world_group } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_spymaster

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no
	only_men = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = deceitful
		in_command = no
		job_marshal = { martial = 10 }
		NOR = {
			is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
			martial = 7
			has_character_modifier = deceitful_martial
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits360 #Appear to be X
		any_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				OR = { martial = 10 trait = honest }
				NOT = { has_job_title = job_marshal }
			}
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = deceitful_neg years = 5 }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = deceitful_martial duration = 730 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits360 #setup job for the blame.
		job_marshal = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = deceitful_neg years = 5 } }
	}
}

# traits.380 Offend another courtier of a trait we dont have
character_event = {
	id = traits.380
	desc = EVTDESCtraits380
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = honest
		in_command = no
		NOR = { trait = shy is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			NOR = { trait = honest is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = gluttonous NOT = { ROOT = { trait = gluttonous } } }
				AND = { trait = slothful NOT = { ROOT = { trait = slothful } } }
				AND = { trait = greedy NOT = { ROOT = { trait = greedy } } }
				AND = { trait = envious NOT = { ROOT = { trait = envious } } }
				AND = { trait = lustful NOT = { ROOT = { trait = lustful } } }
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 5 trait = cruel }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits380
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				NOR = { trait = honest is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				OR = {
					AND = { trait = gluttonous NOT = { ROOT = { trait = gluttonous } } }
					AND = { trait = slothful NOT = { ROOT = { trait = slothful } } }
					AND = { trait = greedy NOT = { ROOT = { trait = greedy } } }
					AND = { trait = envious NOT = { ROOT = { trait = envious } } }
					AND = { trait = lustful NOT = { ROOT = { trait = lustful } } }
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = traits.381 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits381 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = honest_neg years = 10 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits380
		random = {
			chance = 15
			add_trait = stressed
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.282 } }
		}
	}
}

# traits.400 Craven faints at the sight of blood
character_event = {
	id = traits.400
	desc = EVTDESCtraits400
	picture = GFX_evt_feast

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = craven
		in_command = no
		NOR = { trait = hunter is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		any_courtier = {
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { trait = hunter trait = brave NOT = { trait = craven } }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = lustful }

		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = charitable }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits400
		prestige = -10
	}
}

# traits.405 Craven
character_event = {
	id = traits.405
	desc = EVTDESCtraits405
	picture = GFX_evt_bishop

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		trait = craven
		OR = { NOT = { demesne_size = 1 } tier = count }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			prisoner = no
			OR = { is_priest = yes has_job_title = job_spiritual }
			religion_group = old_world_group
			NOR = { character = ROOT trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { trait = craven NOT = { trait = brave } }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = wroth }

		modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = kind }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = humble }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits405
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				OR = { is_priest = yes has_job_title = job_spiritual }
				religion_group = old_world_group
				NOR = { character = ROOT trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				OR = { trait = craven NOT = { trait = brave } }
			}
			character_event = { id = traits.406 days = 1 random = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits406 }
		}
	}
}

### Brave positive ###
# traits.420 Brave positive (on_five_year_pulse)
character_event = {
	id = traits.420
	desc = EVTDESCtraits420
	picture = GFX_evt_hunt
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	only_men = yes
	capable_only = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = brave
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 20
			is_female = no
			prisoner = no
			NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = incapable character = ROOT }
			NAND = { trait = brave martial = 7 }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.3 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = proud }

		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = humble }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = chaste }
	}
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 20
				is_female = no
				NOR = {
					character = ROOT
					trait = incapable
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					AND = { trait = brave martial = 7 }
				}
				prisoner = no
			}
			save_event_target_as = nagging_courtier
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits420
		event_target:nagging_courtier = {
			character_event = { id = traits.421 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits421 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits420
		event_target:nagging_courtier = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = ROOT years = 1 }
		}
	}
}

			}

				NOR = {
					trait = incapable
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					trait = ambitious
					trait = diligent
				}
			}

		}

			NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = incapable character = ROOT }
			OR = { trait = envious trait = just trait = gregarious }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.3 trait = greedy }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = proud }

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = patient }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = humble }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits511
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				in_command = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = incapable character = ROOT }
				OR = { trait = envious trait = just trait = gregarious }
			}
			save_event_target_as = tooltip_text_target
			character_event = { id = traits.512 days = 1 random = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits512 }
		}
	}
}

# traits.535 Just
character_event = {
	id = traits.535
	desc = EVTDESCtraits535
	picture = GFX_evt_throne_room

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = just
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			in_command = no
			prisoner = no
			NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = incapable }
			opinion = { who = ROOT value = 30 }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = envious }
		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = wroth }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				prisoner = no
				NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = incapable }
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 30 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = farmer_dispute_courtier
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits535
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		event_target:farmer_dispute_courtier = {
			set_character_flag = farmer_wrong
			character_event = { id = traits.536 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits536 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits535
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		event_target:farmer_dispute_courtier = {
			set_character_flag = farmer_correct
			character_event = { id = traits.536 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits536 }
		}
	}
}

# traits.570 Zealous negative
character_event = {
	id = traits.570
	desc = EVTDESCtraits570
	picture = GFX_evt_bishop

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = zealous
		in_command = no
		OR = {
			spouse = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				religion_group = old_world_group
				NOT = { trait = zealous }
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 100 }
			}
			any_child = {
				age = 15
				NOT = { trait = zealous }
				religion_group = old_world_group
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 100 }
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = envious }

		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = kind }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_child = {
			limit = {
				age = 15
				NOT = { trait = zealous }
				religion_group = old_world_group
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 100 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = nonzealous_child
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits570
		trigger = {
			spouse = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				NOT = { trait = zealous }
				religion_group = old_world_group
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 100 }
			}
		}
		spouse = {
			set_character_flag = show_in_option
			clr_character_flag = show_in_option
			character_event = { id = traits.571 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits571 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits570
		trigger = { event_target:nonzealous_child = { always = yes } }
		event_target:nonzealous_child = {
			set_character_flag = show_in_option
			clr_character_flag = show_in_option
			character_event = { id = traits.571 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPBtraits571 }
		}
	}
}

			trait = creature_human

			age = 35
		}

			imprison = ROOT

		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				OR = { trait = zealous is_priest = yes }
				NOR = { character = ROOT trait = incapable }

### CELIBATE ###

# traits.620 Celibate
character_event = {
	id = traits.620
	desc = EVTDESCtraits620
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes
	is_married = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = celibate
		in_command = no
		fertility = 0.4
		has_lover = no
		OR = { trait = chaste piety = 50 trait = humble }
		spouse = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_abroad = no
			prisoner = no
			has_lover = no
			is_pregnant = no
			OR = { trait = lustful trait = hedonist NAND = { trait = chaste trait = celibate } }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = wroth }

		modifier = { factor = 2 diplomacy = 8 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits620
		piety = 10
		spouse = {
			character_event = { id = traits.621 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits621 }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_spurned who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits620
		remove_trait = celibate
		piety = -10
		spouse = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } }
				add_lover = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits620
		trigger = {
			fertility = 0.5
			spouse = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				fertility = 0.5
				OR = {
					reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 30 }
					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 30 }
				}
			}
		}
		remove_trait = celibate
		piety = -10
		spouse = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } }
				add_lover = ROOT
			}
		}
		random = { chance = 20 spouse = { impregnate_cuckoo = ROOT } }
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESCtraits621

	}
}

# traits.625 Celibate: Advice
character_event = {
	id = traits.625
	desc = EVTDESCtraits625
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 27
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		trait = celibate

		OR = { trait = chaste NOT = { trait = lustful } piety = 30 }
		any_vassal = {
			trait = chaste
			age = 16
			NOR = { age = 25 is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = celibate trait = hedonist }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = gregarious }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_vassal = {
			limit = {
				trait = chaste
				age = 16
				NOR = { age = 25 is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = celibate trait = hedonist }
			}
			save_event_target_as = chaste_vassal
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits625
		event_target:chaste_vassal = {
			character_event = { id = traits.626 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits626 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits625
		event_target:chaste_vassal = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits625
		tooltip_info = zealous
		trigger = { trait = zealous }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } } add_trait = paranoid }
		event_target:chaste_vassal = {
			add_trait = paranoid
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.275 } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_horrified who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# traits.640 Scholar: Gain learning
character_event = {
	id = traits.640
	desc = EVTDESCtraits640
	picture = GFX_evt_library

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = scholar
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes learning = 15 trait = slothful trait = content }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { learning = 10 trait = scholar }
			has_job_action = no
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }

		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = wroth }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				OR = { learning = 10 trait = scholar }
				has_job_action = no
			}
			save_event_target_as = my_courtier
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits640 #Demand
		piety = -10
		event_target:my_courtier = {
			character_event = { id = traits.641 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits641 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits640 #Ask nicely
		prestige = -10
		event_target:my_courtier = {
			set_character_flag = asked_scholar
			character_event = { id = traits.641 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits641 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits640 #Bribe
		scaled_wealth = -0.10
		event_target:my_courtier = {
			set_character_flag = bribed_scholar
			character_event = { id = traits.641 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits641 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraits640 #Ignore
		add_trait = content
	}
}

# traits.648 Scholar: ROOT
character_event = {
	id = traits.648
	desc = EVTDESCtraits648
	picture = GFX_evt_family

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits648
		trigger = { has_character_flag = embarrassed_child }
		clr_character_flag = embarrassed_child
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_embarrassed who = ROOT years = 2 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits648
		trigger = { has_character_flag = delighted_child }
		clr_character_flag = delighted_child
		FROM = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_delighted who = ROOT years = 2 }
			if = {
				limit = { learning = 6 NOT = { trait = ambitious } }
				random_list = {
					40 = {
						add_trait = ambitious
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.278 #Notify Ambitious
							}
							ROOT = {
								character_event = {
									id = traitnote.178 #Notify Child is Ambitious
								}
							}
						}
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = slothful
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.251 #Notify Slothful
							}
							ROOT = {
								character_event = {
									id = traitnote.151 #Notify Child is Slothful
								}
							}
						}
					}
					40 = {
						add_trait = diligent
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.256 #Notify Diligent
							}
							ROOT = {
								character_event = {
									id = traitnote.156 #Notify Child is Diligent
								}
							}
						}
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = content
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.279 #Notify Content
							}
							ROOT = {
								character_event = {
									id = traitnote.179 #Notify Child is Content
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { learning = 6 } }
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						add_trait = ambitious
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.278 #Notify Ambitious
							}
							ROOT = {
								character_event = {
									id = traitnote.178 #Notify Child is Ambitious
								}
							}
						}
					}
					40 = {
						add_trait = slothful
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.251 #Notify Slothful
							}
							ROOT = {
								character_event = {
									id = traitnote.151 #Notify Child is Slothful
								}
							}
						}
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = diligent
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.256 #Notify Diligent
							}
							ROOT = {
								character_event = {
									id = traitnote.156 #Notify Child is Diligent
								}
							}
						}
					}
					40 = {
						add_trait = content
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.279 #Notify Content
							}
							ROOT = {
								character_event = {
									id = traitnote.179 #Notify Child is Content

							}
						}

# traits.650 Scholar: The Intervention
character_event = {
	id = traits.650
	desc = EVTDESCtraits650
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = scholar
		in_command = no
		OR = { trait = zealous trait = diligent trait = ambitious }
		OR = {
			is_married = yes
			any_courtier = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				NOT = { trait = scholar }
				OR = { trait = envious trait = paranoid trait = wroth }
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 learning = 10 }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = wroth }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits650
		trigger = { is_married = yes }
		spouse = { character_event = { id = traits.651 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits651 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits650
		add_trait = content
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits650
		trigger = {
			is_married = no
			any_courtier = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				NOT = { trait = scholar }
				OR = { trait = envious trait = paranoid trait = wroth }
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 25 }
			}
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				NOT = { trait = scholar }
				OR = { trait = envious trait = paranoid trait = wroth }
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
			}

# traits.655 Gardener: Flower Power
character_event = {
	id = traits.655
	desc = EVTDESCtraits655
	picture = GFX_evt_garden

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = gardener
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes has_character_modifier = flower_power }
		OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = gregarious }
		spouse = { is_alive = yes is_abroad = no prisoner = no }
		any_courtier = {
			NOT = { spouse = { character = ROOT } }
			is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			is_no_females_race = no
			OR = {
				AND = { is_woman_trigger = yes is_pregnant = no }
				ROOT = { is_pregnant = no is_woman_trigger = yes }
			}
			age = 20
			is_abroad = no
			prisoner = no
			NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes age = 40 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT value = 30 }
			reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 30 }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = lustful }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = chaste }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits655
		spouse = { character_event = { id = traits.656 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPAtraits656 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits655
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { spouse = { character = ROOT } }
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				is_no_females_race = no
				OR = {
					AND = { is_woman_trigger = yes is_pregnant = no }
					ROOT = { is_pregnant = no is_woman_trigger = yes }
				}
				age = 20
				NOR = { age = 40 is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				is_abroad = no
				prisoner = no
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 30 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 30 }
			}
			character_event = { id = traits.656 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPBtraits656 }
		}
		random = {
			chance = 10
			spouse = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_idiot who = ROOT years = 1 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits655
		add_character_modifier = { name = flower_power duration = 30 }
	}
}

# traits.660 Gardener: Gain stewardship
character_event = {
	id = traits.660
	desc = EVTDESCtraits660
	picture = GFX_evt_garden

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = gardener
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes stewardship = 15 }
		OR = { trait = diligent trait = ambitious NOR = { trait = slothful trait = content } }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			in_command = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { learning = 10 trait = gardener diplomacy = 8 }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 learning = 9 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits660 #Demand
		piety = -10
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				OR = { learning = 10 trait = gardener diplomacy = 8 }
			}
			set_character_flag = pressured_scholar
			character_event = { id = traits.661 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits661 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits660 #Ask nicely
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				OR = { learning = 10 trait = gardener diplomacy = 8 }
			}
			set_character_flag = asked_scholar
			character_event = { id = traits.661 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits661 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits660 #Bribe
		scaled_wealth = -0.10
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				OR = { learning = 10 trait = gardener diplomacy = 8 }
			}
			set_character_flag = bribed_scholar
			character_event = { id = traits.661 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits661 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraits660 #Ignore
		add_trait = content
	}
}
