# traits.663 Gardener: Spend too much money?
character_event = {
	id = traits.663
	desc = EVTDESCtraits663
	picture = GFX_evt_garden

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = gardener
		in_command = no
		OR = { trait = charitable NOT = { trait = greedy } }
		OR = {
			is_married = yes
			any_courtier = { age = 16 in_command = no trait = greedy stewardship = 8 }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 stewardship = 10 }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = slothful }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits663
		trigger = { is_married = yes }
		spouse = { character_event = { id = traits.664 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits664 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits663
		prestige = 10
		random_list = {
			70 = { }
			20 = {
				add_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.288 #Notify Depressed
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				remove_trait = gardener
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.289 #Notify remove Gardener
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits663
		trigger = { any_courtier = { age = 16 in_command = no trait = greedy stewardship = 8 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.15
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { age = 16 in_command = no trait = greedy stewardship = 8 }
			character_event = { id = traits.665 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits665 }
		}
	}
}

					character_event = {
						days = 3
						id = traitnote.280 #Notify Wounded
					}
				}
				opinion = { modifier = opinion_irritated who = FROM years = 1 }
				FROM = { set_character_flag = beehive_wounded }
			}
			20 = {
				give_ill_effect = yes
				opinion = { modifier = opinion_irritated who = FROM years = 1 }
				FROM = { set_character_flag = beehive_ill }
			}
			10 = { change_stewardship = 1 }
		}
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traits.770 } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_grateful who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits669
		FROM = {
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_coward who = ROOT years = 1 }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traits.771 } }
		}
	}

		}

		}

		}

	}

		name = EVTOPTCtraits671

		FROM = {

		}
	}

	prisoner = no

		in_command = no

			age = 16
			in_command = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { is_priest = yes has_job_title = job_spiritual }
			religion = ROOT
			piety = 50
			has_job_action = no
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = cynical }
	}

			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no

				OR = { is_priest = yes has_job_title = job_spiritual }
				religion = ROOT
				piety = 50
				has_job_action = no
			}

		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits675

	}
}

# traits.685 Mystic: Alchemy - Start
character_event = {
	id = traits.685
	desc = EVTDESCtraits685
	picture = GFX_evt_library

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = mystic
		in_command = no
		OR = { trait = quick trait = genius learning = 8 }
		OR = { trait = diligent trait = ambitious NOR = { trait = slothful trait = content } }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 stewardship = 8 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 martial = 10 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits685
		set_character_flag = fiddle_alchemy
		character_event = { id = traits.686 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPAtraits686 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits685
		set_character_flag = research_alchemy
		character_event = { id = traits.686 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPBtraits686 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits685
		random_list = {
			70 = { }
			15 = { add_trait = stressed }
			15 = { remove_trait = mystic }
		}
	}
}

# traits.686 Mystic: fiddle with alchemy
character_event = {
	id = traits.686
	desc = EVTDESCtraits686
	picture = GFX_evt_library

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits686
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = fiddle_alchemy
				NOT = { has_character_flag = research_alchemy }
			}
			random_list = {
				70 = { character_event = { id = traits.687 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits687 } }
				30 = { character_event = { id = traits.688 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits688 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = research_alchemy
				NOT = { has_character_flag = fiddle_alchemy }
			}
			random_list = {
				50 = { character_event = { id = traits.687 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits687 } }
				50 = { character_event = { id = traits.688 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits688 } }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = fiddle_alchemy
		clr_character_flag = research_alchemy
	}
}

# traits.690 Mystic: Cure for illness - Start
character_event = {
	id = traits.690
	desc = EVTDESCtraits690
	picture = GFX_evt_illness

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	lacks_dlc = "Reapers"
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = mystic
		in_command = no
		OR = { trait = quick trait = genius learning = 10 }
		is_ill = yes
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 stewardship = 8 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 martial = 10 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits690
		random_list = {
			70 = { character_event = { id = traits.691 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits691 } }
			30 = { character_event = { id = traits.692 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits692 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits690
		random_list = {
			10 = { }
			10 = {
				add_trait = pneumonic
				hidden_effect = {
					if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = pneumonic }
				}
				nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
			}
			33 = {
				remove_trait = ill
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.283 #Notify remove ill
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { trait = zealous } }
					add_trait = zealous
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							days = 3
							id = traitnote.269 #Notify Zealous
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits690
		random = { chance = 10 remove_trait = ill }
	}
}

character_event = {

	desc = EVTDESCtraits693
	picture = GFX_evt_illness

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	lacks_dlc = "Reapers"
	war = no

	trigger = {
		trait = mystic
		in_command = no
		is_ill = no
		has_character_modifier = the_alchemist
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			in_command = no
			prisoner = no
			OR = { has_epidemic = yes is_ill = yes }
			NOT = { family = ROOT }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 stewardship = 8 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 martial = 10 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits693
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				OR = { has_epidemic = yes is_ill = yes }
				NOT = { family = ROOT }
			}
			character_event = { id = traits.694 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits694 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits693
		trigger = {
			any_child = { is_abroad = no prisoner = no OR = { has_epidemic = yes is_ill = yes } }
		}
		any_child = {
			limit = { is_abroad = no prisoner = no OR = { has_epidemic = yes is_ill = yes } }
			character_event = { id = traits.694 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits694 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits693
		trigger = {
			spouse = {
				is_abroad = no
				prisoner = no
				is_alive = yes
				OR = { has_epidemic = yes is_ill = yes }
			}
		}
		spouse = { character_event = { id = traits.694 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits694 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraits693
		random_list = {
			60 = { }
			20 = { if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = berserker } } add_trait = craven } }
			20 = { remove_trait = mystic }
		}
	}
}

				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits694
		random = {
			chance = 25

			add_trait = depressed
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.288 #Notify depressed
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# traits.700 Impaler: New torture equipment - Start
character_event = {
	id = traits.700
	desc = EVTDESCtraits700
	picture = GFX_evt_torture

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = impaler
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_brazen_bull #Handled by special decision.
			}
		}
		OR = { trait = wroth trait = deceitful trait = cynical }
		OR = {
			is_married = yes
			any_courtier = { age = 16 in_command = no trait = greedy stewardship = 8 }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 stewardship = 8 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { learning = 6 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits700
		trigger = { is_married = yes }
		scaled_wealth = -0.25
		add_character_modifier = { name = brazen_bull duration = 1825 }
		spouse = { character_event = { id = traits.701 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits701 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits700
		prestige = -10
		random = {
			chance = 10

			add_trait = depressed
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.288 #Notify Depressed
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits700
		trigger = { any_courtier = { age = 16 in_command = no trait = greedy stewardship = 8 } }
		scaled_wealth = -0.25
		clr_character_flag = gold_ten
		add_character_modifier = { name = brazen_bull duration = 1825 }
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { age = 16 in_command = no trait = greedy stewardship = 8 }
			character_event = { id = traits.701 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits701 }
		}
	}
}

	only_playable = yes

	prisoner = no
	war = no

		trait = impaler
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes intrigue = 15 }
		OR = { trait = diligent trait = ambitious NAND = { trait = slothful trait = content } }
		num_of_prisoners = 1
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			in_command = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { learning = 10 trait = impaler intrigue = 8 }
			OR = { trait = wroth martial = 10 }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 learning = 9 }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				OR = { learning = 10 trait = impaler diplomacy = 8 }
				OR = { trait = wroth martial = 10 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = impaler_teacher
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits705 #Demand
		piety = -10

			set_character_flag = pressured_teacher

# traits.710 Impaler
character_event = {
	id = traits.710
	desc = EVTDESCtraits710
	picture = GFX_evt_torture

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = impaler
		in_command = no
		num_of_prisoners = 2
		any_courtier = {
			prisoner = yes
			is_adult = yes
			has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
			is_female = no
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = kind }
		modifier = { factor = 10 has_ambition = obj_atone_for_sins }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = yes
				is_adult = yes
				has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
				is_female = no
			}
			save_event_target_as = confession_target
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits710
		event_target:confession_target = {
			character_event = { id = traits.711 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits711 }
		}
		cancel_ambition = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits710
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = brazen_bull }
		event_target:confession_target = {
			set_character_flag = tortured_by_brazenbull
			character_event = { id = traits.711 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits711 }
		}
		cancel_ambition = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits710
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.268 #Notify Kind
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = charitable } }
			random = {
				chance = 20

				add_trait = charitable
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.273 #Notify Charitable
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_ambition = obj_atone_for_sins } }
			random = {
				chance = 10

				remove_trait = impaler
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.291 #Notify remove Impaler
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_ambition = obj_atone_for_sins }
			remove_trait = impaler
			remove_trait = cruel
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = traits.720
	desc = EVTDESCtraits720
	picture = GFX_evt_melee

	only_playable = yes
	only_men = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

		trait = duelist
		in_command = no
		NOR = {
			is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
			has_character_modifier = averted_duel
			trait = slothful
			trait = content
		}
		any_courtier = {
			age = 20
			is_female = no
			is_abroad = no
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { martial = 10 trait = duelist }
			has_job_action = no
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }

		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = wroth }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 20
				is_female = no
				is_abroad = no
				prisoner = no
				OR = { martial = 10 trait = duelist }
			}
			save_event_target_as = duelist_partner
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits720 #Demand
		piety = -10
		event_target:duelist_partner = {
			set_character_flag = pressured_duelist
			character_event = { id = traits.721 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits721 }
		}

# traits.725 Duelist
character_event = {
	id = traits.725
	desc = EVTDESCtraits725
	picture = GFX_evt_melee

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	max_age = 65
	only_men = yes
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = duelist
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes has_character_modifier = averted_duel }
		any_courtier = {
			is_councillor = no
			age = 20
			is_female = no
			OR = { trait = duelist martial = 9 }
			NOR = {
				is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
				age = 65
				character = ROOT
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 }
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_councillor = no
				age = 20
				is_female = no
				OR = { trait = duelist martial = 9 }
				NOR = {
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					age = 65
					character = ROOT
					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 }
					reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 }
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = duelist_opponent
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits725
		event_target:duelist_opponent = {
			character_event = { id = traits.726 days = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits726 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits725
		prestige = -10
		random = {
			chance = 25

			remove_trait = duelist
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.292 #Notify
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:duelist_opponent = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_coward who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits725
		trigger = {
			job_marshal = {
				age = 20
				prisoner = no
				is_abroad = no
				martial = 6
				can_be_maimed_trigger = yes
				NOT = { trait = wounded }
			}
		}
		random = {
			chance = 10

			remove_trait = duelist
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.292 #Notify
				}
			}
		}
		job_marshal = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_glad who = ROOT years = 5 }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { martial = 7 } }
				random_list = {
					40 = { add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
					40 = { add_trait = wounded }
					10 = { event_target:duelist_opponent = { add_trait = wounded } }
					10 = { event_target:duelist_opponent = { add_maimed_trait_effect = yes } }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { martial = 12 }
				random_list = {
					20 = { add_trait = wounded }
					30 = { event_target:duelist_opponent = { add_maimed_trait_effect = yes } }
					40 = { event_target:duelist_opponent = { add_trait = wounded } }
					10 = { add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# traits.730 Duelist
character_event = {
	id = traits.730
	desc = EVTDESCtraits730
	picture = GFX_evt_melee

	only_playable = yes
	only_men = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = duelist
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes has_character_modifier = averted_duel }
		OR = { trait = brave NOT = { trait = craven } }
		OR = {
			is_married = yes
			any_courtier = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = duelist }
				OR = { trait = craven martial = 6 }
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wounded }

		modifier = { factor = 0.1 is_woman_trigger = yes }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = slothful }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits730
		trigger = { is_married = yes }
		spouse = { character_event = { id = traits.731 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits731 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits730
		trigger = { is_married = no }
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = duelist }
				OR = { trait = craven martial = 6 }
			}
			character_event = { id = traits.732 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits732 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits730
		if = {
			limit = { is_married = yes spouse = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no is_abroad = no } }
			spouse = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_glad who = ROOT years = 5 } }
		}
		random = {
			chance = 20

			remove_trait = duelist
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.292 #Notify
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# traits.735 Duelist: The unusual duel - Start
character_event = {
	id = traits.735
	desc = EVTDESCtraits735
	picture = GFX_evt_melee

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	max_age = 65
	only_men = yes
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = duelist
		in_command = no
		OR = { tier = duke tier = count }
		NOR = {
			is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
			has_character_modifier = averted_duel
			has_character_modifier = master_duelist
		}
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_lord = {
				is_female = no
				age = 20
				OR = { tier = duke tier = count }
				can_be_maimed_trigger = yes
				NOR = {
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					age = 65
					opinion = { who = ROOT value = -10 }
					trait = wounded
					trait = infirm
					trait = incapable
				}
				OR = { trait = duelist martial = 9 }
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = craven }
	}

	immediate = {
		top_liege = {
			random_realm_province = {
				limit = {
					any_province_lord = {
						is_female = no
						age = 20
						OR = { tier = duke tier = count }
						can_be_maimed_trigger = yes
						NOR = {
							is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
							age = 65
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = -10 }
							trait = wounded
							trait = infirm
							trait = incapable
						}
						OR = { trait = duelist martial = 9 }
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = duel_location
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits735
		event_target:duel_location = {
			random_province_lord = {
				limit = {
					is_female = no
					age = 20
					OR = { tier = duke tier = count }
					can_be_maimed_trigger = yes
					NOR = {
						is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
						trait = wounded
						trait = infirm
						trait = incapable
						age = 65
						opinion = { who = ROOT value = -10 }
					}
					OR = { trait = duelist martial = 9 }
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = traits.736 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits736 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits735
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = craven trait = berserker } } add_trait = craven }
		random = {
			chance = 20

			remove_trait = duelist
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.292 #Notify
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# traits.740 Hunter: Gain martial
character_event = {
	id = traits.740
	desc = EVTDESCtraits740
	picture = GFX_evt_hunt

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = hunter
		in_command = no
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes martial = 15 trait = slothful trait = craven }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			in_command = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { martial = 10 trait = hunter }
			has_job_action = no
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }

		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = wroth }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				OR = { martial = 10 trait = hunter }
				has_job_action = no
			}
			save_event_target_as = fellow_hunter
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits740 #Demand
		piety = -10
		event_target:fellow_hunter = {
			set_character_flag = pressured_hunter
			character_event = { id = traits.741 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits741 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits740 #Ask nicely
		prestige = -10
		event_target:fellow_hunter = {
			set_character_flag = asked_hunter
			character_event = { id = traits.741 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits741 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits740 #Bribe
		scaled_wealth = -0.10
		event_target:fellow_hunter = {
			set_character_flag = bribed_hunter
			character_event = { id = traits.741 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits741 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraits740 #Ignore
		add_trait = content
		random = {
			chance = 20

			remove_trait = hunter
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.293 #Notify
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

# traits.750 Hunter
character_event = {
	id = traits.750
	desc = EVTDESCtraits750
	picture = GFX_evt_hunt

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOR = {
			religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
			is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
			has_character_modifier = master_hunter
		}
		trait = hunter
		in_command = no
		OR = { trait = brave NOT = { trait = craven } }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			in_command = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { has_job_title = job_marshal has_job_title = job_spymaster }
			has_job_action = no
			OR = { trait = ambitious trait = diligent }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

				}
			}
			25 = {
				add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
				FROM = {
					set_character_flag = courtier_maimed
					character_event = { id = traits.753 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits753 }
				}
			}
			25 = {
				prestige = 15
				FROM = {
					set_character_flag = courtier_unharmed
					character_event = { id = traits.753 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits753 }
				}
			}
			25 = {
				FROM = {
					set_character_flag = courtier_unharmed
					character_event = { id = traits.753 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits753 }
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_delighted who = ROOT years = 1 } }

character_event = {

# traits.760 Poet: Gain diplomacy
character_event = {
	id = traits.760
	desc = EVTDESCtraits760
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	ai = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = poet
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = { trait = charitable trait = gregarious NAND = { diplomacy = 15 trait = content } }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious }

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }

		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = wroth }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits760 #Demand
		piety = -10
		create_character = {
			attributes = { diplomacy = 10 }
			dynasty = random
			trait = poet
			age = 30
			female = no
			has_nickname = nick_the_troubadour
		}
		new_character = {
			# Set Racial Trait
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
			set_character_flag = pressured_poet
			character_event = { id = traits.761 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits761 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits760 #Ask nicely
		prestige = -10
		create_character = {
			attributes = { diplomacy = 10 }
			dynasty = random
			trait = poet
			age = 30
			female = no
			has_nickname = nick_the_troubadour
		}
		new_character = {
			# Set Racial Trait
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
			set_character_flag = asked_poet
			character_event = { id = traits.761 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits761 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits760 #Bribe
		scaled_wealth = -0.10
		create_character = {
			attributes = { diplomacy = 10 }
			dynasty = random
			trait = poet
			age = 30
			female = no
			has_nickname = nick_the_troubadour
		}
		new_character = {
			# Set Racial Trait
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
			set_character_flag = bribed_poet
			character_event = { id = traits.761 days = 2 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits761 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraits760 #Ignore
		add_trait = content
		create_character = {
			attributes = { diplomacy = 10 }
			dynasty = random
			trait = poet
			age = 30
			female = no
			has_nickname = nick_the_troubadour
		}
	}
}

	prisoner = no

		in_command = no
		trait = poet
		diplomacy = 6
		NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = wroth }
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			in_command = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { trait = gluttonous trait = greedy trait = wroth }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits765
		piety = -10
		add_trait = lustful
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits765
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				OR = { trait = gluttonous trait = greedy trait = wroth }
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_upset who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cynical } } add_trait = cynical }
		if = { limit = { trait = kind } remove_trait = kind }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits765
		piety = 10
		any_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				trait = zealous
				OR = { trait = chaste trait = humble piety = 100 }
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_approves who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = zealous } } add_trait = zealous }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraits765
		add_trait = wroth
	}
}

# traits.775 Poet: Courtier asks for a poem
character_event = {
	id = traits.775
	desc = EVTDESCtraits775
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		trait = poet
		has_lover = no
		any_courtier = { age = 20 prisoner = no has_lover = no NOT = { trait = poet } }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { age = 20 prisoner = no has_lover = no NOT = { trait = poet } }
			save_event_target_as = poem_customer
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits775
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = poetry_timer duration = 430 hidden = yes }
		}
		add_trait = charitable
		event_target:poem_customer = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_grateful who = ROOT years = 5 }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				add_character_modifier = { name = used_poetry_timer duration = 430 hidden = yes }
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						ROOT = { is_married = yes is_female = no }
						is_woman_trigger = yes
						NOR = { trait = homosexual trait = celibate age = 30 }
					}
					ROOT = { spouse = { character_event = { id = traits.776 } } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits775
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = poetry_timer duration = 430 hidden = yes }
		}
		treasury = 10
		event_target:poem_customer = {
			hidden_tooltip = {
				add_character_modifier = { name = used_poetry_timer duration = 430 hidden = yes }
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_mildly_disappointed who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits775
		prestige = 10
		event_target:poem_customer = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

			limit = {
				is_woman_trigger = yes
				NOR = { trait = celibate trait = homosexual }
				prisoner = no
				is_abroad = no
				age = 20
				has_lover = no
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = sweetheart
		}
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				has_character_modifier = poetry_timer
				war = no
				in_command = no
				prisoner = no
			}
			save_event_target_as = poem_author
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits780
		event_target:sweetheart = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } }
				add_lover = ROOT
			}
		}
		random = {
			chance = 10
			event_target:poem_author = {
				character_event = { id = traits.781 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits781 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits780
		prestige = -10
		remove_trait = deceitful
		event_target:sweetheart = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_glad who = ROOT years = 10 } }
	}
}

		factor = 1

					id = traitnote.294 #Notify remove Poet
				}
			}
		}
	}

				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {

					}
				}

		}
	}

# traits.790 Falconer: bring-your-kids-to-hunt day
character_event = {
	id = traits.790
	desc = EVTDESCtraits790
	picture = GFX_evt_falconing

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 20
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	war = no
	is_married = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		trait = falconer
		spouse = { is_alive = yes NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes } }
		num_of_children = 3
		any_child = {
			age = 13
			NOR = { age = 20 is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			is_abroad = no
			prisoner = no
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	immediate = {
		random_child = {
			limit = {
				age = 13
				NOT = { age = 20 is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				is_abroad = no
				prisoner = no
			}
			save_event_target_as = hunting_child
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits790
		event_target:hunting_child = {
			character_event = { id = traits.791 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits791 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits790
		spouse = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = ROOT years = 5 } }
		event_target:hunting_child = {
			character_event = { id = traits.792 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits791 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits790
		spouse = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_angry who = ROOT years = 5 } }
		event_target:hunting_child = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = ROOT years = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraits790
		trigger = {
			spouse = { is_alive = yes trait = falconer }
			event_target:hunting_child = { trait = brave martial = 10 }
		}
		character_event = { id = traits.797 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits797 }
	}
}

character_event = {

	capable_only = yes

					}
				}
			}
			50 = {
				add_trait = wroth
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.254 #Notify Wroth
					}
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:falcon_help = {
			clr_character_flag = sold_falcon
			random = {
				chance = 20

				set_character_flag = killed_falcon
				character_event = { id = traits.805 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPBtraits805 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# traits.810 Vassal does NOT support liege
character_event = {
	id = traits.810
	desc = EVTDESCtraits810
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary

	only_playable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = gregarious
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		any_vassal = {
			prisoner = no
			war = no
			in_command = no
			NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes opinion = { who = ROOT value = -10 } }
		}
		OR = {
			trait = diligent
			trait = ambitious
			NOT = { trait = slothful }
			NOT = { trait = content }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	immediate = {
		random_vassal = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				war = no
				in_command = no
				NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes opinion = { who = ROOT value = -10 } }
			}
			save_event_target_as = nonsupportive_vassal
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits810
		event_target:nonsupportive_vassal = {
			character_event = { id = traits.811 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits811 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits810
		prestige = 10
		event_target:nonsupportive_vassal = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_upset who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# traits.815 Gregarious character's child is shy!
character_event = {
	id = traits.815
	desc = EVTDESCtraits815
	picture = GFX_evt_guardian

	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = gregarious
		in_command = no
		any_child = { is_abroad = no prisoner = no trait = shy }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = trusting }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = paranoid }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = cynical }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_child = {
			limit = { is_abroad = no prisoner = no trait = shy }
			save_event_target_as = shy_child
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits815
		event_target:shy_child = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					remove_trait = shy
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.295 #Nofify Remove Shy
						}
					}
				}
				25 = {
					add_trait = gregarious
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.265 #Nofify Gregarious
						}
					}
				}
				30 = {
					add_trait = paranoid
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.275 #Nofify Paranoid
						}
					}
				}
				20 = {
					add_trait = stressed
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.282 #Nofify Stressed
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits815
		event_target:shy_child = {
			random_list = {
				30 = { }
				25 = {
					add_trait = paranoid
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.275 #Nofify Paranoid
						}
					}
				}
				25 = {
					add_trait = cynical
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.258 #Nofify Cynical
						}
					}
				}
				20 = { change_intrigue = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = cynical } }
			random = {
				chance = 25

				add_trait = cynical
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.258 #Nofify Cynical
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits815
		event_target:shy_child = {
			random_list = {
				20 = { }
				10 = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_unhappy who = ROOT years = 5 } }
				20 = {
					add_trait = depressed
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.288 #Nofify Depressed
						}
					}
				}
				25 = { change_learning = 1 }
				25 = {
					remove_trait = shy
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.295 #Nofify Remove Shy
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = content } }
			random = {
				chance = 25

				add_trait = content
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.279 #Nofify Cynical
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# traits.820 Take care of a courtier?
character_event = {
	id = traits.820
	desc = EVTDESCtraits820
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	prisoner = no
	only_rulers = yes
	capable_only = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		trait = gregarious
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			in_command = no
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			trait = proud
			opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 }
			OR = { trait = shy trait = paranoid trait = cynical }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				in_command = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				trait = proud
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 10 }
				OR = { trait = shy trait = paranoid trait = cynical }
			}
			save_event_target_as = proud_courtier
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits820
		event_target:proud_courtier = {
			character_event = { id = traits.821 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits821 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits820
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = slothful } }
			random = {
				chance = 25

				add_trait = slothful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.251 #Notify Slothful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = slothful NOT = { trait = content } }
			random = {
				chance = 25

				add_trait = content
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.279 #Notify Content
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = content trait = slothful }
			random = {
				chance = 25

				remove_trait = gregarious
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.279 #Notify Remove Gregarious
					}
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:proud_courtier = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_upset who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraits820
		event_target:proud_courtier = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_grateful who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = slothful }
			random = {
				chance = 25

				remove_trait = slothful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.298 #Notify Remove Slothful
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = content }
			random = {
				chance = 25

				remove_trait = content
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.299 #Notify Remove Content
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = traits.830
	desc = EVTDESCtraits830
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		trait = wroth
		any_courtier = {
			age = 16
			prisoner = no
			is_abroad = no
			NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = incapable trait = wroth trait = patient }
			OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable prestige = 60 }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 16
				prisoner = no
				is_abroad = no
				NOR = {
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					trait = incapable
					trait = wroth
					trait = patient
				}
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable prestige = 60 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = wroth_onlooker
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 is_priest = no }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = weak }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = scholarly_theologian }
		modifier = { factor = 0.3 trait = mastermind_theologian }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits830
		event_target:wroth_onlooker = {
			character_event = { id = traits.831 days = 1 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits831 }
		}
	}
}

	id = traits.840
	desc = EVTDESCtraits840
	picture = GFX_evt_jerusalem

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	is_triggered_only = yes
	religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
	war = no

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		trait = brave
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = proud }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = lustful }

		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = humble }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = chaste }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits840
		piety = 15
	}
}

# traits.850 Zealous negative - Ormazd
character_event = {
	id = traits.850
	desc = EVTDESCtraits850
	picture = GFX_evt_jerusalem

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no
	religion_group = ormazd_gods_group

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = zealous
		in_command = no
		OR = {
			spouse = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				NOT = { trait = zealous }
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 100 }
			}
			any_child = { age = 15 NOT = { trait = zealous } opinion = { who = ROOT value = 100 } }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 4.0 trait = gluttonous }
		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = envious }

		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = kind }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits850
		trigger = {
			spouse = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				NOT = { trait = zealous }
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 100 }
			}
		}
		spouse = { character_event = { id = traits.851 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits851 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraits850
		trigger = {
			any_child = { age = 15 NOT = { trait = zealous } opinion = { who = ROOT value = 100 } }
		}
		random_child = {
			limit = { age = 15 NOT = { trait = zealous } opinion = { who = ROOT value = 100 } }
			character_event = { id = traits.851 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits851 }
		}
	}
}

# traits.851 Spouse
character_event = {
	id = traits.851
	desc = EVTDESCtraits851
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAtraits851
			trigger = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes spouse = { character = ROOT } } }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBtraits851
			trigger = { FROM = { is_woman_trigger = no spouse = { character = ROOT } } }
		}
		name = { text = EVTOPTCtraits851 trigger = { father = { character = FROM } } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTDtraits851 trigger = { mother = { character = FROM } } }
		FROM = {
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = zealous_neg who = ROOT years = 2 }
			character_event = { id = traits.852 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraits852 }
		}
	}
}

# traits.855 Zealous negative - Ormazd
character_event = {
	id = traits.855
	desc = EVTDESCtraits855
	picture = GFX_evt_jerusalem

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	war = no
	religion_group = ormazd_gods_group

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		scaled_wealth = 0.5
		trait = zealous
		NOT = { trait = greedy }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 3.0 trait = envious }
		modifier = { factor = 5.0 trait = lustful }

		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = kind }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = charitable }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = honest }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraits855
		scaled_wealth = -0.025
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 5: Life with a trait (namespace traitlife)
# Namespace: traitlife (was 65000 - 65167 in various_traits_events.txt;
#   traitlife.N is the old id minus 64900)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: every character
#
# Small events for the traits that shape a life more than a temper does:
# stress, cruelty, zeal, sin, drink, illness and the marks of birth (short,
# clubfooted, harelip, hunchback, lisp, stutter, inbred, imbecile, ugly,
# gluttonous, slow). A stressed ruler leans on a councillor, a cruel one
# hardens a child or shoots at the court, a drunkard sleeps through the
# sermon, a short ruler meets a halfling troupe, a clubfoot falls down the
# stairs, an ugly one is whispered to be a witch.
#
#   Stress                                traitlife.100 - traitlife.112
#   Cruelty                               traitlife.120 - traitlife.123
#   Zeal and sin                          traitlife.130 - traitlife.137
#   Drink                                 traitlife.140 - traitlife.151
#   Illness                               traitlife.152 - traitlife.157
#   Short                                 traitlife.160 - traitlife.166
#   Clubfoot, harelip, hunchback          traitlife.170 - traitlife.195
#   Lisp, stutter, inbred, imbecile       traitlife.200 - traitlife.226
#   Ugly, gluttonous, slow                traitlife.230 - traitlife.260
#   Ormazd branches                       traitlife.265 - traitlife.267
#
# Fixes: four `break` effects after a saved invincible character became
#   if/else (traitlife.120, 122, 131, 132); the stressed Ormazd scholar
#   (266) checked the dead vanilla group muslim and never fired; the
#   gluttonous doorway (255) lost its vanilla Muslim/Jewish split; God,
#   Jesus, Purgatory, Church, imam, mosque and Allah texts now speak of
#   the gods, Shallya, Morr, temples, magi and Ormazd; the "dwarf" of the
#   Short trait is a halfling or a jester, since Dwarfs are a people here.
# Added: a zealous scholar's tract (135) may, once a lifetime and only if
#   no religious book is held, be bound as Faith Eternal (10%).
# Fired from: on_bi_yearly_pulse random_events (WHG_pulse_on_actions.txt), and
#   from each other and WHG_baptism_events (traitlife.251).
# Loc: localisation/00_WHG_personality_events.csv
###############################################################################

namespace = traitlife

### Stressed ###

# traitlife.100 Stressed character: Command a unit
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.100
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife100
	picture = GFX_evt_crusaders

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16
	war = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = stressed
		in_command = yes
		NOR = {
			has_character_modifier = stressed_commander
			has_character_modifier = misplaced_food
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife100
		add_character_modifier = { name = stressed_commander duration = 365 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife100
		troops = -0.2
	}
}

# traitlife.101 Stressed: Diplomacy
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.101
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife101
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary

	only_men = yes
	capable_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = stressed
		diplomacy = 2
		NOT = { diplomacy = 10 }
		job_chancellor = { diplomacy = 10 }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife101
		random = { chance = 25 change_diplomacy = -1 remove_trait = stressed }
		job_chancellor = {
			opinion = { modifier = trusting_pos who = ROOT years = 5 }
			character_event = { id = traitlife.102 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraitlife102 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife101
	}
}

# traitlife.102 Chancellor gets +diplomacy and relation
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.102
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife102
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife102
		change_diplomacy = 1
	}
}

# traitlife.111 Ruler lashes out in anger to hide internal frustrations
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.111
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife111
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	only_men = yes
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = stressed
		NOT = { trait = kind }
		OR = {
			spouse = { is_abroad = no prisoner = no is_alive = yes NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			any_child = { is_abroad = no prisoner = no is_primary_heir = yes }
			any_courtier = { is_abroad = no prisoner = no is_councillor = yes }
			any_courtier = { is_abroad = no prisoner = no is_councillor = no }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = arbitrary }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife111
		trigger = { is_married = yes }
		random_spouse = {
			character_event = { id = traitlife.112 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPAtraitlife112 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife111
		trigger = { any_child = { is_primary_heir = yes } }
		random_child = {
			limit = { is_primary_heir = yes }
			character_event = { id = traitlife.112 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPBtraitlife112 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraitlife111
		trigger = { any_courtier = { is_councillor = yes } }
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { is_councillor = yes prisoner = no NOT = { trait = incapable } }
			character_event = { id = traitlife.112 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPCtraitlife112 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraitlife111
		trigger = { any_courtier = { is_councillor = no } }
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { is_councillor = no prisoner = no NOT = { trait = incapable } }
			character_event = { id = traitlife.112 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPDtraitlife112 }
		}
	}
}

### Cruel ###

# traitlife.120 Cruel character educates child
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.120
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife120
	picture = GFX_evt_guardian

	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			guardian = { character = ROOT }
			is_weak_trigger = yes
			age = 9
			NOT = { age = 16 }
			can_be_maimed_trigger = yes
			NOT = { trait = brave }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife120 #Try to beat some sense in the child
		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		random_child = {
			limit = {
				guardian = { character = ROOT }
				is_weak_trigger = yes
				age = 9
				NOT = { age = 15 }
				can_be_maimed_trigger = yes
				NOT = { trait = brave }
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_cruel who = ROOT years = 5 }
			random = { chance = 50 add_maimed_trait_effect = yes }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife120 #Put the child out with the wolves
		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		random_child = {
			limit = {
				guardian = { character = ROOT }
				is_weak_trigger = yes
				age = 9
				NOT = { age = 15 }
				can_be_maimed_trigger = yes
				NOT = { trait = brave }
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_mad_at_guardian who = ROOT years = 100 }
			random = { chance = 25 add_trait = brave }
			random = {
				chance = 75
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
				else = { death = { death_reason = death_execution killer = ROOT } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# traitlife.121 Imprisoned character and Cruel ruler
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.121
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife121
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	only_rulers = yes
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = cruel
		any_courtier = { prisoner = yes host = { character = ROOT } }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife121 #Reduce food rations
		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes host = { character = ROOT } }
			random = {
				chance = 25

				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
					add_trait = ill
					hidden_effect = {
						if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = ill }
					}
					nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
				}
				if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } add_symptom_effect = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife121 #Release some rats in cell
		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes host = { character = ROOT } }
			add_character_modifier = { name = rats_in_cell duration = 90 }
		}
	}
}

# traitlife.122 You decide that firing arrows on your court is a good idea
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.122
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife122
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic

	only_rulers = yes
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
		trait = cruel
		in_command = no
		any_courtier = { prisoner = no NOT = { trait = wounded } }
		any_courtier = { prisoner = no age = 20 can_be_maimed_trigger = yes }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife122 #Aim low
		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { prisoner = no NOT = { trait = incapable trait = wounded } }
			add_trait = wounded
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.280 } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_horrified who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife122 #Aim high
		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 20
				prisoner = no
				can_be_maimed_trigger = yes
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
			}
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
					else = { death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown_arrow killer = ROOT } }
				}
				50 = {
					add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.281 } }

					opinion = { modifier = opinion_cruel who = ROOT years = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# traitlife.123 Craven courtier is frightened by ruler
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.123
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife123
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	only_rulers = yes
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = cruel
		in_command = no
		any_courtier = { trait = craven age = 20 }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife123 #Pretend to push them off the walls
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { trait = craven prisoner = no NOT = { trait = incapable } age = 20 }
			random = {
				chance = 33

				add_trait = stressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.282 #Notify stressed
					}
				}
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_dislike who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife123 #Scare them in the night
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { trait = craven prisoner = no age = 20 NOT = { trait = incapable } }
			random = { chance = 50 add_trait = depressed }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_dislike who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
}

### Zealous ###

# traitlife.130 Depressed, Zealous Character
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.130
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife130 #God should not make us feel this...
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news

	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group

	is_triggered_only = yes

		in_command = no
		trait = depressed
		trait = zealous
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife130 #Pray harder
		random_list = {
			50 = { remove_trait = depressed }
			25 = {
				add_trait = stressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.282 #Notify stressed
					}
				}
			}
			25 = { }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife130 #Doubt self
		piety = -5
		random = { chance = 25 remove_trait = zealous }
	}
}

	}

		factor = 1
	}

		random_list = {
			10 = {
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
				else = { death = { death_reason = death_trait } }
			}
			10 = {
				add_trait = pneumonic
				hidden_effect = {
					if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = pneumonic }
				}
			}
			33 = {
				remove_trait = ill
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.283 #Notify remove ill
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife131 #Continue praying
		random = {
			chance = 1

			remove_trait = ill
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.283 #Notify remove ill
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# traitlife.135 Scholar wrote a small religious tract
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.135
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife135
	picture = GFX_evt_bishop

	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		in_command = no
		trait = scholar
		trait = zealous
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife135 #Send to the temples
		trigger = { is_ruler = yes }
		add_character_modifier = { name = religious_tract duration = 3650 }
		if = { # rare: a fair copy is bound for the library, once a lifetime
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = religious_tract_bound }
				NOT = { any_artifact = { has_artifact_flag = about_religion } }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 10
				set_character_flag = religious_tract_bound
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPtraitlife135 }
				add_artifact = faith_eternal
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife135 #Vassal give liege
		trigger = { liege = { age = 1 NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
		piety = 10
		liege = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_religious_tract who = ROOT years = 10 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraitlife135 #Courtier give host
		trigger = { host = { age = 1 NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
		piety = 10
		host = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_religious_tract who = ROOT years = 10 } }
	}
}

# traitlife.136 Lack of sin
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.136
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife136
	picture = GFX_evt_bishop

	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		NOR = {
			trait = lustful
			trait = gluttonous
			trait = slothful
			trait = greedy
			trait = envious
			trait = wroth
			trait = proud
			trait = incapable
		}
		OR = {
			trait = chaste
			trait = temperate
			trait = charitable
			trait = diligent
			trait = kind
			trait = patient
			trait = humble
		}
		trait = zealous
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife136 #Proclaim lack of sin publicly
		prestige = 5
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = no
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				OR = {
					trait = lustful
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = slothful
					trait = greedy
					trait = envious
					trait = wroth
					trait = chaste
					trait = proud
				}
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_bragger who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife136 #Thank the Lord in private
		trigger = { NOT = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group } }
		piety = 1
		add_trait = humble
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraitlife136 #Thank Ormazd in private
		trigger = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
		piety = 1
		add_trait = humble
	}
}

# traitlife.137 Someone is Celibate
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.137
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife137
	picture = GFX_evt_bishop

	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		NOT = { trait = celibate trait = lustful trait = hedonist }
		trait = zealous
		OR = {
			any_courtier = { trait = celibate prisoner = no }
			any_vassal = { trait = celibate prisoner = no }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife137 #ask him/her for advice...
		trigger = { any_courtier = { trait = celibate prisoner = no } }
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { trait = celibate prisoner = no NOT = { trait = incapable } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_flattered who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		random = { chance = 50 add_trait = celibate }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife137 #try to emulate him/her
		trigger = { any_vassal = { trait = celibate prisoner = no } }
		random_vassal = {
			limit = { trait = celibate prisoner = no }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_flattered who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
		random = { chance = 50 add_trait = celibate }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraitlife137
		random = { chance = 25 add_trait = celibate }
	}
}

# traitlife.140 Fine meal
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.140
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife140
	picture = GFX_evt_drunk

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = old_world_group
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = drunkard }
		in_command = no
		OR = { trait = gluttonous trait = slothful }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 5 trait = hedonist }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife140
		add_trait = drunkard
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife140
		if = {
			limit = { trait = gluttonous }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
				add_trait = ill
				hidden_effect = {
					if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = ill }
				}
				nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } add_symptom_effect = yes }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = gluttonous } } add_trait = gluttonous }
	}
}

		has_guardian = yes
		guardian = { religion_group = old_world_group }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife155
		guardian = { character_event = { id = traitlife.156 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraitlife156 } }
	}
}

# traitlife.156 Guardian answers
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.156
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife156
	picture = GFX_evt_illness

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife156 #Yes, you are a sinner that deserves it
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = { factor = 0.01 FROM = { father = { character = ROOT } } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.01
				FROM = {
					father = {
						OR = {
							reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					NOT = { trait = lustful }
					NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
					NOT = { trait = greedy }
					NOT = { trait = slothful }
					NOT = { trait = envious }
					NOT = { trait = wroth }
					NOT = { trait = proud }
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				add_trait = cynical
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.258 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						trait = lustful
						trait = gluttonous
						trait = greedy
						trait = slothful
						trait = envious
						trait = wroth
						trait = proud
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				add_trait = stressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.282 #Notify stressed
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife156 #Don't worry, I'll take care of you
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = just }
			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 10 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 10 NOT = { trait = cruel } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				FROM = {
					father = {
						OR = {
							reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
							opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			random_list = {
				45 = {
					add_trait = kind
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.268 #Notify kind
						}
					}
					ROOT = {
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.168 #Notify guardian: child = kind
							}
						}
					}
				}
				45 = {
					add_trait = charitable
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.273 #Notify charitable
						}
					}
					ROOT = {
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.173 #Notify guardian: child = charitable
							}
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					remove_trait = ill
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.283 #Notify remove ill
						}
					}
					ROOT = {
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.183 #Notify guardian: child = ill
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraitlife156 #Why do you bother me?
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = { factor = 100 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = { factor = 100 trait = cruel }
		}
		FROM = {
			random_list = {
				70 = {
					add_trait = arbitrary
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.266 #Notify arbitrary
						}
					}
					ROOT = {
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.166 #Notify guardian: child = arbitrary
							}
						}
					}
				}
				30 = {
					add_trait = cynical
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.258 #Notify cynical
						}
					}
					ROOT = {
						hidden_tooltip = {
							character_event = {
								id = traitnote.158 #Notify guardian: child = cynical
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDtraitlife156 #God will cure you!
		FROM = {
			random = {
				chance = 10

				remove_trait = ill
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.283 #Notify remove ill
					}
				}
				ROOT = {
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.183 #Notify guardian: child = remove ill
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_tossed_me who = ROOT years = 1 }

	}

			}

		}
		if = { limit = { trait = shy } remove_trait = shy }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = gregarious } NOT = { trait = shy } }
			add_trait = gregarious
		}
	}
}

# traitlife.191 Hunchback: There is always Hope
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.191
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife191
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife191
		create_character = {
			name = Hope
			random_traits = yes
			dynasty = random
			female = yes
			age = 20
			health = 10
			attributes = { stewardship = 8 intrigue = 6 }
		}
		new_character = {
			# Set Racial Trait
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
			set_character_flag = hope_everlasting
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_my_saviour who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
		character_event = { id = traitlife.192 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraitlife192 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife191
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = wroth } } add_trait = wroth }
		if = { limit = { trait = wroth NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		if = {
			limit = { is_ruler = yes }
			create_character = {
				name = Hope
				random_traits = yes
				dynasty = random
				female = yes
				age = 20
				health = 10
				attributes = { stewardship = 8 intrigue = 6 }
			}
			new_character = { imprison = ROOT }
		}
	}
}

				prisoner = no

				has_character_flag = hope_everlasting
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_lover who = ROOT years = 10 }
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_lover who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife192
		add_trait = cynical
		prestige = -5
	}
}

# traitlife.195 Hunchback: Laughter behind your back
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.195
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife195
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news

	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		trait = hunchback
		NOT = { prestige = 50 }
		OR = {
			trait = lustful
			trait = gluttonous
			trait = greedy
			trait = slothful
			trait = envious
			trait = wroth
			trait = proud
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife195
		random_list = {
			40 = { }
			30 = {
				any_courtier = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						NOT = { trait = wroth }
						NOT = { trait = cruel }
					}
					opinion = { modifier = opinion_gained_respect who = ROOT years = 5 }
				}
			}
			30 = {
				add_trait = wounded
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.280 #Notify Wounded
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife195
		random_list = {
			60 = { }
			20 = {
				add_trait = proud
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.261 #Notify Proud
					}
				}
			}
			20 = {
				add_trait = honest
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.262 #Notify Honest
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraitlife195
		prestige = 5
		random_list = {
			50 = { }
			25 = {
				add_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.288 #Notify Depressed
					}
				}
			}
			25 = {
				add_trait = shy
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.263 #Notify Shy
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = traitlife.201
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife201
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	only_rulers = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		trait = lisp
		NOT = { trait = stutter }
		NOT = { trait = harelip }
		any_vassal = {
			war = no
			in_command = no
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { trait = harelip }
			NOT = { trait = stutter }
			NOT = { trait = lisp }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife201
		prestige = 5
		random_vassal = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = harelip } NOT = { trait = stutter } NOT = { trait = lisp } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_disgruntled who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife201
		prestige = -5
		random_vassal = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = harelip } NOT = { trait = stutter } NOT = { trait = lisp } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_forgive who = ROOT years = 5 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraitlife201
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				add_trait = deceitful
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.255 #Notify Deceitful
					}
				}
			}
			30 = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { trait = berserker } }
					add_trait = craven
					hidden_tooltip = {
						character_event = {
							id = traitnote.257 #Notify Craven
						}
					}
				}
			}
			20 = { }
		}
	}
}

# traitlife.202 Lisp: Hard to understand
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.202
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife202
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		in_command = no
		trait = lisp
		NOT = { trait = shy }
		NOT = { trait = gregarious }
		NOT = { trait = stutter }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife202
		random = {
			chance = 30

			add_trait = gregarious
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.265 #Notify Gregarious
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife202
		random_list = {
			40 = { }
			20 = {
				add_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.288 #Notify Depressed
					}
				}
			}
			2 = {
				add_trait = patient
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.264 #Notify Patient
					}
				}
			}
			20 = {
				add_trait = humble
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.260 #Notify Humble
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCtraitlife202
		random = {
			chance = 1

			remove_trait = lisp
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = traitnote.302 #Notify remove Lisp
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = zealous } } add_trait = zealous }
	}
}

# traitlife.245 Ugly: Ugly Duckling
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.245
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife245
	picture = GFX_evt_child_talking

	min_age = 12
	max_age = 20
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes
		in_command = no
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife245 #No longer ugly.
		random_list = {
			80 = {
				if = { limit = { trait = ugly } remove_trait = ugly }
				if = { limit = { trait = uncouth } remove_trait = uncouth }
			}
			20 = {
				if = { limit = { trait = ugly } remove_trait = ugly add_trait = fair }
				if = { limit = { trait = uncouth } remove_trait = uncouth add_trait = groomed }
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.301 #Notify Fair
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# traitlife.250 Gluttonous: Crushes horse
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.250
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife250
	picture = GFX_evt_courier

	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	war = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = gluttonous
		in_command = no
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 10 has_ambition = obj_atone_for_sins }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife250 #Admission of fatness
		trigger = { NOT = { has_ambition = obj_atone_for_sins } }
		random_list = {
			50 = { }
			25 = {
				add_trait = shy
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.263 #Notify Shy
					}
				}
			}
			25 = {
				remove_trait = gluttonous
				lose_weight_small_effect = yes
				weight_trait_check_effect = yes
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitlife.251 #Notify Remove Gluttonous
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife255 #Admission of fatness - Buddhist ambition
		trigger = { has_ambition = obj_atone_for_sins }
		remove_trait = gluttonous
		lose_weight_small_effect = yes
		weight_trait_check_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = {
				id = traitlife.251 #Notify Remove Gluttonous
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife250 #Get me another horse.
		random_list = {
			80 = { }
			20 = {
				add_trait = wounded
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.280 #Notify Wounded
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# traitlife.266 Stressed Ormazd: Learning
character_event = {
	id = traitlife.266
	desc = EVTDESCtraitlife266
	picture = GFX_evt_jerusalem_captured_muslims

	capable_only = yes
	only_rulers = yes
	prisoner = no
	religion_group = ormazd_gods_group

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = stressed
		learning = 2
		NOT = { learning = 10 }
		job_spiritual = { learning = 10 }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAtraitlife109
		random = { chance = 25 change_learning = -1 remove_trait = stressed }
		job_spiritual = {
			opinion = { modifier = trusting_pos who = ROOT years = 5 }
			character_event = { id = traitlife.267 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPtraitlife110 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBtraitlife109
		piety = -5
	}
}
